Bake time on the host: Temperature 0.5-1.6 ms, Rain now 0.5-0.6 ms (2.2-2.6 ms before #95); S3 (0.9.61 log, 2026-10-12, before #95): Temperature 0.51-0.54 s, Rain now 1.68-1.69 s, Rain forecast 0.21-0.22, Wind 0.43-0.44, Next days 0.16-0.17, Sun 0.44-0.45, Air 0.30 s of black (the `tempface: baked parts 7 ... face F ...` log line). OTA hold unchanged: the cut is a flag, a held or hidden job parks as before.
- **Face dots**: the design system's dots (canvas AT3: r 4, on the r 206 arc at the bottom, 16 px apart, the shown face #FFFFFF, the others #4A4A4A), one per face in the list, centred (two now, at x 225 and 241). They are baked into the background (`wx_paint_face_dots`, both scenes): no LVGL object, no per-frame cost, they fade with the face in M8 and shift with the burn-in step. A face change is always a full scene bake, so the white dot is always the shown face. With six faces the positions are the canvas's (host test).
- **Cost** (`idf.py size`, origin/main 4f3f7ee → this branch, same machine and worktree, IDF 5.5.5):

  | | origin/main | 0.9.53 | Δ |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Image (`cinder.bin`) | 1,609,872 B | 1,610,896 B | +1,024 |
  | Flash `.text` / `.rodata` | 1,079,556 / 426,844 B | 1,080,576 / 426,844 B | +1,020 / 0 |
  | IRAM `.text` | 15,356 B | 15,356 B | 0 |
  | DIRAM static (`.data` / `.bss`) | 101,935 B (23,464 / 15,248) | 101,935 B (23,464 / 15,248) | 0 (`wx_job_t`'s `cut` fits its padding) |
  | PSRAM `.bss` | 49,964 B | 49,980 B | +16 (`s_faces`, `s_face`) |
  | PSRAM heap, weather page shown / hidden | 903,330 B / 0 | same | 0 |
  | `stack_walk.py` | | `lvgl` 8,592 (margin 1,904), `wxbake` 2,528 of 4,096 (margin 1,568; the dots' disc under `wx_bake`) | `lvgl` 0 |

- **Tests**: `test_faces.c` (list order, next and previous wrapping, several detents, a huge count, an id outside the list; open and turn, a full turn round the list, a net-zero flick from auto pinning the face; the turn base: the face on screen during a fade out, the requested face before anything landed, the pick over a stale face without buffers, two quick detents, unknown faces; auto, picked Temperature while wet (no rain data), picked Rain now while dry, without a chance, a clamped chance, old, stale and no data, picked Rain now while wet equals auto, a dry storm; bake kinds: face changes, dry ↔ wet and dry ↔ light rain as full scenes, the chance on the dry face as marker or ring only, no intensity ratchet across dry; dot positions against the canvas for 6 and centred for 2, dot colours and radius in the baked background of five cases), `test_wx_job.c` (`test_user_switch_cuts`: detach at once with `WX_DO_ARRIVE`, no fade in, a later data change still fades; a cut mid fade out; a cut behind a running ring bake, behind a running scene bake and before the first bake lands, each landing without a paint, and a landing painted when the turns end on the face that lands; `test_turn_face`: `wx_job_face` before the first bake, through a fade out and the black of a weather-driven bake, after a hide and reopen before that bake lands, after a release; a pending cut meeting a ring-only change dropped while the fade in keeps stepping; a cut dropped when nothing changes; a page open clears it), `test_pages.c` (weather gets detents, pushes and longs; its input hook), `run.sh` (update resolves the picked face with `wx_temp_face` and records it, show opens on auto, the only `wx_job_cut` is behind `wx_faces_turn`, `act` resets the arrival clock on `WX_DO_ARRIVE`, `page_weather_input` turns faces, `paint` keeps the sheets hidden on the dry face). Mutants killed: `wx_job_cut` as a no-op, the fade out kept on a cut, the cut never dropped, a show that keeps the cut, no `WX_DO_ARRIVE`, `dry` missing from the scene compare or the ratchet, no face compare in `wx_temp_bake_kind`, no dry fill, a Temperature pick that keeps the rain data, no dots, no wrap below 0, an open that keeps the pick; after review: a landing painted under a pending cut, a cut taken on a ring-only change, a pick set only when the face changes, a base from the requested face; after the re-check: the landing check without its scene test, a turn base that needs the face attached; #74: a reopen that keeps the last visit's face. `test_turn_face` also reopens during a running ring bake (the buffers stay attached, the last visit's face is on screen): a `wx_job_show(true)` that clears `seen` even when attached fails it.
- **On the knob** (to do): turn on the weather page both ways and spin it (the black between faces: record the bake log's ms here); push and turn still changes pages; the dots' look and size against AT3; the dry Rain now face against AR2; a weather change on an unpicked page still fades; `psram_largest` unchanged after many switches.

## Rain forecast face (#75, #34 part 2, 0.9.54)

The third weather face (canvases AD3, AD4, AD5, [`face-design.md`](face-design.md)): how long until the rain starts, or stops while it rains, on a 12 h clock dial. Same pipeline as the other weather faces: `wx_job` bake on `wxbake`, images only while shown, OTA hold, M8 fade on a weather-driven change, cut and arrival on a turn, face dots baked, burn-in shift. **Not checked on the knob yet.**

- **Data**: no new field. Ember's `rain` block (`spells` for the next 12 h, `until`), `now.rain`, `utc_offset` and the observation's age, already parsed for the Rain now face (Ember#350, `parse_detail`). The knob's clock is `wx_knob_now` (`observed_at` + the age at receipt + the time since, as Rain now uses it).
- **Face order and auto face** (`wx_faces.h`, `wx_temp.c`): `WXF_TEMP`, `WXF_RAIN`, `WXF_CAST`. A turn reaches the forecast on any weather. The auto face (each page open, and until the user turns) is Rain now while wet, else the forecast when the data is fresh, it is dry and the next spell starts within 60 min (`WX_CAST_SOON_S`; kept up to 90 min once the forecast shows, `WX_CAST_KEEP_S`, so a start revised around 60 min does not flip the face), else Temperature. So the page opens on "45 min" when rain is close and never on a forecast hours away. Rain starting then switches to Rain now through M8's fade, as before.
- **Selection** (`components/weather/wx_cast.c`, pure, host-tested):

  | State | Value | Sub-line | Dial |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Dry, a spell within 12 h | countdown to its start | "light rain at 17:00" (word in #5C9CE0) | lit arcs, drop slides from now to the start, label "17" or "15:15" at the start |
  | Wet with `until` | countdown to `until` | "rain until 15:40" | drop slides to just before the stop, label at the stop |
  | Wet without `until` (the spell runs to the data's edge, or Ember's merged dry gap) | "--" grey | "Rain", "Heavy snow", ... | arcs, no drop or label |
  | Dry, no spell in the next 12 h (`spells: []`, or only spells that ended) | "12+ h" in #BDBDBD | "No rain for 12 h" | unlit cells, marker at now |
  | No `rain` block or no `utc_offset` | "--" grey | "No forecast" | grey cells, no marker |
  | Stale, old (> 30 min), disabled, no data | "--" grey | the observation's age offline, else none | grey cells |

  - Wet is Rain now's rule: `now.rain > 0` or a spell covering now, until `until` passes on the knob's clock. A passed `until` ends only the wet spell it belongs to: a later spell covering now (starting at or after that `until`, from a view not refreshed since) keeps it wet, with the word and no countdown, and Rain now does the same (it no longer shows a past "until HH:MM"), so the auto face and both faces agree (Codex review of #76). The next spell is the earliest start after now, whatever the order in the view.
  - Dry for 12 h (0.9.55, user report on 0.9.54: "rain forecast is empty"; decided by the maintainer's delegate): a real answer, not missing data, so a full face instead of the grey "--" the face showed until 0.9.54. `wx_cast_from` sets `dry`, variant 1 and a count of 24 half hours; the value counts up 0, ½, ... 11½ and lands on "12+" (`wx_cast_step_text`; '+' is in `font_digits_96`'s subset), unit "h", both in the sub-line grey #BDBDBD (`WX_CAST_DRY_RGB`: calm, and not the blue of a countdown or the #5A5A5A of no data). Hero: AD4's white cloud, breathing as on the light face, without its drop sheet (`set_cast` keeps the sheets hidden while `dry`; the sheet is still baked into the still buffer, as on the dry Rain now face). No drop slide and no dial label (no time to name). `dry` is part of `wx_cast_same_scene`, so rain coming into or leaving the 12 h fades through black as any scene change, and the dry face never re-bakes as time passes (no arcs). `wx_cast_value` gives the value, unit and colour for every state; "--" in #5A5A5A without a unit stays for missing data only (no rain block or offset, stale, old, no clock, no data) and for wet without `until` (the stop is unknown). Never the auto face (`wx_cast_soon` needs a next spell). Cost: image +144 B (`.text` +124, `.rodata` +16), DIRAM and PSRAM `.bss` unchanged (`dry` fits the struct's padding), `stack_walk.py` `lvgl` 8,592 and `wxbake` 2,528 unchanged (origin/main 8fa89c0 → 0.9.55, same machine and path length). Steady areas: none on most frames, the cloud box (180×106) on the 121 of 900 frames where the cloud steps (~2.6k px avg), less than the light face.
  - Countdown (`wx_cast_round`): under 57.5 min in 5-min steps rounded half up, at least "5 min"; from there in half hours ("1 h", "1½ h", "2½ h", up to "12 h"). The count-up on arrival steps through the same values (0, ½, 1, ... or 0, 5, 10, ...) on the Temperature face's ease (`wx_count_step`).
  - Variant (the canvas's three boards) by the intensity of the spell counted to (wet: the spell covering now, else `now.rain`): 1 light (AD4, one white cloud), 2 moderate (AD5, lighter filled clouds from both sides), 3 heavy (AD3, grey layered clouds from both sides); snow by the spell's kind. While it stays wet with the same kind a falling peak keeps the shown variant (as Rain now's ratchet), so the face does not fade through black as the heavy part of a spell passes.
  - Dial: the canvas's 12 hour cells (27° each, 1.5° gaps, #262626), lit in the spell's intensity colour (light #4F81B7, moderate #5C9CE0, heavy #3D7BF5) from now to the whole hour before now + 12 h, per hour cell. The clip at now steps every 5 min (`WX_CAST_STEP_S`): while a spell is under way the dial re-bakes at most every 5 min (a ring bake, no blink). The far end steps by the hour, so a dry dial with a spell running past now + 12 h re-bakes once an hour, not every 5 min; when a spell starts at or after that hour step (in the last hour Ember lists), every spell is cut at the 5-min step after now + 12 h instead, at most 2.5° past the marker, so every listed spell keeps its arc and a spell crossing the hour keeps its rain past it (that window re-bakes every 5 min). The marker moves with the minute. Clock angle = local time mod 12 h. A spell that has ended (its end at or before now) draws no arc (#82, 0.9.65): the clip at the 5-min step before now used to keep a spell ending off the 5-min grid lit for up to 5 min after it ended (a spell ending 14:33, knob at 14:34: a 3-min arc from 14:30). Ember's spells end on 5-min steps, so it could not happen with today's server; the dial now re-bakes once when such a spell ends instead of at the next 5-min step (for the last spell that bake is the wet-to-dry scene change).
- **Re-bake** (`wx_temp_bake_kind`): a face change, the variant or snow is a full scene (fade through black, as M8); the arcs (a spell revised, the 5-min clip while wet) a ring bake; the countdown, marker, sub-line and label never bake.
- **Rendering** (`wx_paint.c` bake, `main/temp_face.c` LVGL):
  - Background (the shared 466² RGB565 buffer): the face dots and the dial, painted into the ring strip (`wx_paint_ring_cast`, the same annulus runs as the gauge ring, now one rasteriser with a pixel function per face) and copied in, so a dial change is a ring bake.
  - Per side (light: one; moderate and heavy: left and right) one RGB565A8 cloud sprite with the rim fade baked (`wx_paint_clouds`, the canvas's closed cloud path since 0.9.63, see "Cloud shape" under the Temperature face; the existing tones are the canvas's: white #E6ECF2/#C4CCD4, light #8E96A0/#A8B0BA, dark #555D68 front and #3A4048 back with #6A7480; fade from r 180 or 160 to 195, clipped to the r 195 box) and one drop sheet: an A8 strip of the sheet's width × two tiles (the canvas's A and B patterns, opt2's `RAIN_SPECS`: tiles 40 or 60 rows, 2.0 s, 0.8 s, 0.93 s per tile), shown twice in a clipping container and recoloured (#5C9CE0, snow #D0DCE8 as dots at half speed), with black A8 ramps over the top and bottom 8 % (the canvas's sheet fade). All carved out of the still-cloud buffer (`wx_cast_layout`): light 49,364 B, moderate 88,700 B, heavy 165,412 B of its 201,312 B.
  - Motion after the arrival: the sheets step at 15 fps; each cloud breathes ±4 px (the canvas's periods: 8 s, the right moderate cloud 9 s and 4 s ahead, the right heavy one 8.5 s) in 1 px steps, about 2 px/s; the heavy clouds pulse 1 → 0.7 → 1 over 3.2 s in 4 levels each way (`WX_CAST_GLOOM_STEPS`).
  - One box per side (`wx_cast_layout`'s `box`: the cloud over its breathe range and its sheet, the sides never overlap): on a frame where the cloud steps or pulses, `set_cast` invalidates an invisible object over that box, so the cloud's and the sheet's own invalidations join into it (each lies inside); otherwise only the sheet's box redraws.
  - Arrival (on page open and on a cut): the marker sits at now; the drop (the canvas's teardrop, #5C9CE0 with a 2 px black stroke) slides along the ring from now to the start on cubic-bezier(0.45, 0, 0.25, 1) over 0.96 s at 30 fps, fading in over 0.18 s and out by 1.2 s. It is one 34² RGB565A8 canvas repainted at each new angle (`wx_paint_drop`, ¼° steps, on the LVGL task like the mercury; the tail's half-width table is a constant, pinned to the bezier by `test_cast.c`; against the old per-paint bisection the drop's alpha differs by ±1 at most; host ~6 µs per paint), one small box. The clouds fade in at 15 fps (as the Temperature face's sprites), the sheets open over 0.96-1.14 s, the dial label (Montserrat 24, #5C9CE0) fades in over 0.96-1.32 s, the number counts up, then the sub-line fades in.
  - Dial label: placed at its canvas anchor (r 198 edge-aligned at the sides, r 188 centred at top and bottom), but hidden when its box would come within 2 px of the sub-line's box (`wx_cast_label_place`, measured with the label's and the sub-line's real widths, recolour markup excluded). That happens for start or stop times near 3:30-4:10 and 7:50-8:30 (and +12 h) with the longer sub-lines: 1,022 of 17,280 cases in `test_cast_label.c` (every minute of the day, wet and dry, three intensities, rain and snow). The sub-line names the same time, so nothing is lost.
  - Text: the countdown in `font_digits_96` (it has ½), its unit "h" or "min" in 30 px beside it, the pair centred, both #5C9CE0 (dry for 12 h: both #BDBDBD; missing data: "--" in #5A5A5A, no unit); the sub-line uses LVGL's recolour markup for the coloured word (`lv_label_set_recolor`, on only for this face).
- **Areas/frame** (host model, `test_cast.c`, 900 frames per variant): steady state light 1 area (the sheet, 142×36 px; the cloud box 180×106 when the cloud steps, 121 of 900 frames), avg 7.0k px per frame; moderate 2 areas, avg 10.1k px; heavy 2 areas, avg 24.4k px (the pulse redraws a side box of up to 29k px about 2.5 times a second per side). Rain now: 3 areas, 35k px every frame. Arrival: number, drop, clouds (15 fps), label and later the sub-line: ≤ 5 areas.
- **Bake time**: host (M-series, -O1) full scene 1.3-2.1 ms, dial alone 0.5-1.0 ms (`test_cast.c` prints them). S3 0.21-0.22 s (0.9.61 log, `tempface: baked parts ... face 2 ...`).
- **Cost** (`idf.py size`, origin/main 77e27c4 → this branch, same machine and path length, IDF 5.5.5, `sdkconfig` from `sdkconfig.defaults`):

  | | origin/main | 0.9.54 | Δ |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Image (`cinder.bin`) | 1,610,896 B | 1,626,496 B | +15,600 |
  | Flash `.text` / `.rodata` | 1,080,576 / 426,844 B | 1,093,540 / 429,484 B | +12,964 / +2,640 |
  | IRAM `.text` | 15,356 B | 15,356 B | 0 |
  | DIRAM static (`.data` / `.bss`) | 101,935 B (23,464 / 15,248) | 101,999 B (23,464 / 15,312) | +64 (the new object and buffer pointers) |
  | PSRAM `.bss` | 49,980 B | 50,492 B | +512 (`wx_cast_t` in the job's keys, layout, shown state) |
  | PSRAM heap, weather page shown / hidden | 903,330 B / 0 | 906,798 B / 0 | +3,468 (the drop canvas); the forecast layers live in the still-cloud buffer |
  | Internal heap (LVGL objects, for life) | | 16 more objects (2 boxes, 2 sheet containers, 4 strips, 4 ramps, 2 clouds, the drop, the dial label) | estimate ~3-4 KB, not measured |
  | `stack_walk.py` | `lvgl` 8,592 (margin 1,904), `wxbake` 2,528 (margin 1,568) | same | 0 |

- **Deviations from AD3-AD5**: the forecast is its own face in the turn list (third dot), not a second view of Rain (no left-edge view dots); the clouds fade in instead of sliding in from the sides (as on the Temperature face: a sprite with a baked rim fade cannot slide under a fixed mask); the side sheets stay put while their cloud breathes; a heavy side is one sprite, so its back cloud pulses with the front one, and the pulse has 4 levels each way instead of 24; the dial's lit arcs show at full strength from the start (the canvas glows them from 0.6); the unit takes the value's colour (the canvas's #9A9A9A, as with Rain now's "%"); the countdown shows "1 h" from 57.5 min and rounds half up (the canvas's `countdown()` shows "60 min" there and rounds half to even); the dial label hides where it would touch the sub-line; the states the canvases do not show (wet, no forecast, snow) are mine; dry for 12 h ("12+ h", AD4's cloud without drops, "No rain for 12 h") is the delegate's decision above.
- **Tests**: `test_cast.c` (the three canvases: countdown, variant, sub-line, label text and place, marker, drop travel 78°, 24°, 48° and the lit arc; rounding and the count-up steps; wet with and without `until`, wet by `now.rain` alone and snow from the condition, a passed `until` against a later covering spell (also through `wx_rain_from` and the auto face), a spell ending exactly now, a spell ending off the 5-min grid unlit once it ended (#82), a one-minute spell, a pause in a spell, `until` passed on the knob's clock, dry for 12 h, no rain block, no offset, stale, old, no clock, no data, snow, spell order, the ratchet and its limits; arcs cut at now + 12 h, the 5-min clip, dry arcs steady as time passes; the auto rule and its hysteresis (exactly 60 and 90 min, and no hold with Temperature or Rain now on screen), picks over and onto the forecast; a wet spell getting heavier; the dry far end not re-baking every 5 min, a spell starting in the last hour before now + 12 h or exactly on the hour step still lit, its exact far end, the 5-min cadence in that window, and a spell crossing the hour step lit past it; the three canvases' labels show), `test_faces.c` (three faces: order, wrapping, turns and the turn base, dots for the forecast faces), `test_fixtures.c` (the forecast from Ember's goldens: `view_faces.json` wet, 2 h to the stop and both arcs; `view_faces_met.json` 2 h to the moderate spell at 14:00 and not the auto face; `view_faces_polar_dry.json` dry for 12 h: "12+ h" in #BDBDBD, the light cloud, "No rain for 12 h"); `test_cast.c`'s `test_dry` (0.9.55: the count-up steps and "12+", unit and colour, a spell that ended or ends exactly now, the countdown's blue, "--" grey for wet without `until`, no rain block, stale and no data, never the auto face) and `test_bake_kind` (dry ↔ light rain a full scene with the same cloud, dry as time passes no bake). ## Wind face (#78, #34 part 3, 0.9.56)

The fourth weather face (canvases AW2, AW3, AW4, [`face-design.md`](face-design.md)): the wind speed on a 0..50+ km/h gauge with a windsock that stands up and flutters faster as the wind rises. Same pipeline as the other weather faces: `wx_job` bake on `wxbake`, images only while shown, OTA hold, M8 fade on a weather-driven change, cut and arrival on a turn, face dots baked, burn-in shift. **Not checked on the knob yet.**

- **Data**: Ember's `now.wind_kmh` and `now.wind_dir` (Ember#350, in the goldens since 0.9.51), now parsed in `parse_detail` (`wx_obs_t` `has_wind`/`wind_kmh`, `has_wind_dir`/`wind_dir`). `wind_kmh` 0..999 rounded, `wind_dir` 0..360 rounded with 360 read as 0 (north); missing, not a number or out of range is unknown. `gust_kmh` and `today.gust_max_kmh` are not read: the canvases show none (AW2's first draft had "SW, gusts 34"; the final one shows the band name), so no face needs them yet. No protocol change.
- **Face order and auto face** (`wx_faces.h`, `wx_temp.c`): `WXF_TEMP`, `WXF_RAIN`, `WXF_CAST`, `WXF_WIND`; four face dots. A turn reaches it on any weather. The auto face is Rain now while wet, else Rain forecast when rain is due within the hour, else Wind when the data is fresh and the sustained wind is at least 39 km/h (Beaufort 6, "Strong breeze", AW4's band; `WX_WIND_AUTO_KMH`), kept down to 34 km/h once the Wind face shows (`WX_WIND_KEEP_KMH`, so a wind wavering around 39 does not flip the face), else Temperature. Rain wins over wind because it asks for an action now; a breezy day never takes the page from the temperature. Gusts do not trigger it: the face shows the sustained wind, and opening on "25 km/h" because of a gust would not explain itself.
- **Selection** (`components/weather/wx_wind.c`, pure, host-tested):

  | State | Value | Sub-line | Gauge | Sock |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | Fresh, `wind_kmh` known | speed, "km/h" (#9A9A9A) | band name in its colour, " • ", 8-point direction ("Gentle breeze • SW") | band colours, marker at min(speed, 50) / 50 | the speed's level, band colour, A-B-C-B |
  | Calm (0 km/h) | "0" | "Calm" (no direction) | | limp, swaying |
  | No `wind_dir` | | band name only | | |
  | No `wind_kmh`, stale, old (> 30 min), disabled, no data | "--" grey, no unit | none (offline: the observation's age) | grey track, no marker, no end labels | limp, still, grey |

  - Bands (the canvas's `BANDS`, Beaufort in km/h, upper bounds inclusive): Calm 0, Light air ≤ 5 #9AA4B0, Light breeze ≤ 11 #4FD6CC, Gentle breeze ≤ 19 #8ACF7A, Moderate breeze ≤ 28 #C9D25A, Fresh breeze ≤ 38 #E8D44A, Strong breeze ≤ 49 #F08A3A, Near gale ≤ 61 #FF5050, then #D03060 for Gale ≤ 74, and (added past the canvas, which stops at "Gale") Strong gale ≤ 88, Storm ≤ 102, Violent storm ≤ 117, Hurricane. The value counts up on the Temperature face's ease (`wx_count_step`), each step in its own band colour, and the sock takes the same colour (the canvas's `wxsockfill`), so a gale passes through every band as it arrives. A speed over 50 shows as it is (to 999); the marker stays at the "50+" end.
  - Direction: where the wind comes from, 8 points (N, NE, ... NW; 22.5° either side, so 338-22 is N). 16 points would make "Moderate breeze • NNW" the widest line; the canvases use 2-letter points.
  - Levels (`wx_wind_level`): 0 limp (AW3) below 12 km/h (calm to light breeze), 1 gentle (AW2) 12..28 (gentle and moderate breeze), 2 straight out (AW4) from 29 (fresh breeze up; the canvas's sock is fully out from 28). A level change is a full scene bake (fade through black), so a shown level is kept while the speed stays within 3 km/h past its edge (`WX_WIND_HYST`, `wx_wind_level_keep`, as the cloud bands): gentle shown holds from 9 to 31 km/h.
- **Re-bake** (`wx_temp_bake_kind`): a face change, a level change or the data coming or going is a full scene; the speed, the colour, the direction (also coming or going) and the marker never bake.
- **Rendering** (`wx_paint.c` bake, `main/temp_face.c` LVGL):
  - Background: the pole (7 px, round knob on top, the 22 px arm to the hoop, the 48 px base on y 205, #9AA4B0) for the level, the face dots and the gauge ring (`wx_paint_ring_wind`: the annulus with a LUT of the band colour at each angle; grey #262626 without data). End labels "0" (#9AA4B0) and "50+" (#FF5050) in `font_face_36`.
  - The sock: per level the canvas's 1.9× geometry (`sock_geometry` at the canvas's 4, 18 and 45 km/h, the hoop and droop of `hero_sock_face`) as five quads, and per pose (A: −ang, scale (1 − stretch, 1.02); B: none; C: +ang, (1 + stretch, 0.96); ang/stretch 5°/0.012, 4°/0.02, 3°/0.03) two A8 planes of the sock box: the light stripes and the darker stripes 2 and 4. 4×4 supersampled; the fill plane holds the light samples divided by what the dark plane leaves uncovered (0 where only dark covers), so dark drawn over fill counts every sample once: the stripes meet without a seam, and the dark stripes' outer edges stay as soft against the background as the light ones (PR #79 review). Two canvases show them recoloured: the fill in the band colour, the dark stripes over it in 55 % of it (`wx_wind_dark`, the canvas's `shade()`). So a colour change is two style calls, no bake; a pose change swaps both buffers.
  - Streaks (1, 3, 4 rows by level: the canvas's rows) are A8 sprites of the 4 px round-capped line, recoloured #9AA4B0, moved and faded per frame as `wxstreak` (−24 → +16 → +56 px, opacity 0 → 0.75 → 0, in the canvas's `steps()` per half period). The strong level's two leaves (#8ACF7A) fly 140 px with the canvas's `wxleaf` path, steps and fades; their turning is 12 baked rotations (15° apart; the leaf is the same turned by 180°), picked per frame.
  - All of it lives in the still-cloud buffer (`wx_wind_layout`: limp 47,214 B, gentle 79,530 B, strong 49,788 B of 201,312 B); no new allocation.
  - Motion: the pose cycle A-B-C-B every 10, 5 or 2 frames at 15 fps (667 / 333 / 133 ms per pose, the design's numbers), the streaks and leaves at 15 fps. No live rotation, scale or skew. One redraw box per level (`area`: the sock, every streak's and leaf's travel): on a frame where anything in it changes, `set_wind` invalidates an invisible object over the box, so the objects' own invalidations join into it. Without data, and during M8's fade (`fo < 255`), the sock rests on pose B with no streaks or leaves.
  - Arrival (page open and cut): the marker sweeps, the number counts up and the sock and streaks fade in at 15 fps (as the Temperature face's sprites), then the sub-line fades in.
  - Text: the speed in `font_digits_96` with "km/h" in 30 px beside it, the pair centred; the sub-line uses LVGL's recolour markup (on for this face and the forecast). The bullet is U+2022, which the built-in Montserrat 30 has; it has no U+00B7 (the canvas's middle dot). The widest sub-line, "Moderate breeze • NW", is 338 px, 20 px from the ring's inner edge on each side at its baseline (`test_wind_label.c` requires 16).
- **Areas/frame** (host model, `test_wind.c`, 900 frames per level): 1 area at 15 fps: limp 120×140 (16,800 px) on 880 of 900 frames, gentle 150×126 (18,900 px) and strong 168×112 (18,816 px) on every frame. Rain now: 3 areas, 35k px every frame; Rain forecast heavy: 2 areas, avg 24.4k px. Arrival: the number, the marker and the box, then the sub-line: ≤ 4 areas.
- **Bake time**: host (M-series, -O1) full scene 1.2-1.9 ms over 5 runs of the three levels, one outlier at 3.0 ms (`test_wind.c` prints it; the sock planes dominate). S3 0.43-0.44 s (0.9.61 log, `tempface: baked parts 7 ... face 3 ...`).
- **Cost** (`idf.py size`, origin/main 2f76eb0 (0.9.55) → this branch, same worktree, IDF 5.5.5, `sdkconfig` from `sdkconfig.defaults`):

  | | origin/main | 0.9.56 | Δ |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Image (`cinder.bin`) | 1,626,640 B | 1,637,152 B | +10,512 |
  | Flash `.text` / `.rodata` | 1,093,664 / 429,500 B | 1,102,824 / 430,860 B | +9,160 / +1,360 |
  | IRAM `.text` | 15,356 B | 15,356 B | 0 |
  | DIRAM static (`.data` / `.bss`) | 101,999 B (23,464 / 15,312) | 102,071 B (23,464 / 15,384) | +72 (the new object pointers) |
  | PSRAM `.bss` | 50,492 B | 50,844 B | +352 (`wx_wind_t` in the job's keys, layout, shown state) |
  | PSRAM heap, weather page shown / hidden | 906,798 B / 0 | same | 0: the wind layers live in the still-cloud buffer |
  | Internal heap (LVGL objects, for life) | | 9 more objects (the box, sock, stripes, 4 streaks, 2 leaves) | estimate ~2 KB, not measured |
  | `stack_walk.py` | `lvgl` 8,592 (margin 1,904), `wxbake` 2,528 (margin 1,568) | same | 0 (`wx_paint_sock` takes its quads from `bake_wind`, so `sinf` is never called under its frame; inside it the path was 1,904 and the margin 1,424) |

- **Deviations from AW2-AW4**: the wind face is the fourth dot (the canvas's "3 of 6" counts faces not built yet); the sock's shape is one of three levels (the canvas bends it continuously with the speed; the three shapes are the canvases' own at 4, 18 and 45 km/h) and keeps its level 3 km/h past an edge; the sock fades in on arrival instead of swinging down from 75° (a live rotation and scale); during M8's fade and without data it rests on pose B; the gauge's colour follows the band at each angle instead of the canvas's 12 solid segments (a segment straddling a band edge differs by a few degrees); the bullet is U+2022, not U+00B7 (not in Montserrat 30); band names past "Gale" (Strong gale, Storm, Violent storm, Hurricane) and the states the canvases do not show (calm, no direction, no data) are mine; gusts are not shown (see Data).
- **Tests**: `test_wind.c` (bands and colours at every edge to 999, the dark shade against the canvas's `shade()`; compass points at every edge and wrap; levels and the keep at every edge and from both sides; `wx_wind_from` for the three canvases, calm, no direction, over 50, clamping, a negative speed, direction wrap, the longest sub-line in its buffer, the kept level and when it is not kept; no data: no `wind_kmh`, stale, old, disabled, invalid, NULL; the auto rule at 39/38, the hold at 34/33, no hold under another face, Rain now and the forecast winning, stale and no wind never auto; picks onto and away from Wind clear the other faces' data; bake kinds: speed and direction never bake, the kept level, a level change, data going, a face change; every pose's quads against the canvas's `_xf()` points; the A-B-C-B cycle at 667/333/133 ms; streak offsets and opacities on the canvas's steps and delays; the leaves' path, turn, fade and loop; layouts fit the still buffer, the sock box holds every pose, every streak and leaf stays in the area for 80 s, the area off the ring and above the value; areas per frame; the bake: pole, arm, base, knob, width, the face dot, the gauge colours by band and grey without data, each stripe in its plane, soft edges, poses A and C differ, every leaf rotation baked, the streak's 4 px line and caps, the leaf's turn direction), `test_wind_label.c` (with Montserrat 30 from LVGL: every sub-line from 0 to 130 km/h in 8 directions has all its glyphs and the widest keeps 16 px from the ring; "km/h"), `test_faces.c` (four faces: order, wrapping, turns, the turn base, dots for the Wind face with and without data), `test_fixtures.c` (`view_faces.json` 21 km/h SW "Moderate breeze" while Rain now wins; `view_faces_met.json` 15 km/h from 200° "Gentle breeze • S" without gusts; fractional, 360°, negative, too large, string and missing keys; legacy views have no wind). `run.sh`: the wind attaches on a scene apply, its canvases detach with the others and attach only from the still buffer, `paint` keeps the baked wind while fading out, the motion stops without data or while fading, one box redraws. Mutants killed (58 tried): level edges and keep bounds, the dark factor, the pose order, the auto and keep thresholds, the scene compare (have, level), the streak phase sign, opacity and travel, calm with a direction, the shown-level conditions, the unclamped gauge, the pose angle and scale, the leaf turn, fade and position, a band edge, stale data, the hold, the cleared wind on a pick, the bake kind, the shown wind on a pick, a direction coming or going treated as a scene change, the stripe parity, the fill plane not divided by the uncovered part (hard dark edges, or a dark seam between stripes), the pole width, the gauge LUT direction, the streak radius and row, the leaf rotation sign and count, the box margins, the face dot, the direction wrap in the parser and in `wx_wind_from`, the parser's ranges, the frame for "still". Two survivors are equivalent: the compass rounding `+45` vs `+44` (2d + 45 is odd, never a multiple of 90) and the crossing test counting left instead of right.
- **On the knob** (to do): turn to the Wind face on a calm and a windy day (live, or the fixtures through a scratch server only with the user's OK): compare with AW2-AW4 (snapshot); the pose cycle and the streaks at each level; the stats log for the one box (ms per frame, against Rain now's three); the bake log's ms for a full scene; the recoloured sub-line and the bullet; the auto face at 39 km/h.

## Next days face (#83, #34 part 4, 0.9.57)

The fifth weather face (canvas AN1, [`face-design.md`](face-design.md) row "Next days"): up to three rows, each a day name, a condition icon, the chance of precipitation under the icon on wet days, the low, a range bar on one temperature axis shared by the rows, and the high. Same pipeline as the other weather faces: `wx_job` bake on `wxbake`, images only while shown, OTA hold, M8 fade on a forecast change, cut and arrival on a turn, face dots baked, burn-in shift. **Not checked on the knob yet.**

- **Data**: Ember's `weather.days` (Ember#350, in the goldens since 0.9.51), now parsed in `parse_detail` (`parse_days`, `wx_obs_t` `day_n`/`day[WX_DAYS]`): per day `t` (local midnight, Unix s), the condition resolved on the knob from `cond`/`code` with `wx_look_from(provider, cond, code, 0)` (the current mapping; a day is never night, so MET's `_night` symbols draw the day icon), `min_c`/`max_c` (-99..99, rounded) and `pop` (0..100, integer; anything else unknown). Each kept day also records its end (`end`): Ember's `end` (Ember#434, v0.53.1: the next true local midnight in the location's zone, 23 h or 25 h after `t` on a DST day) when it is an integer 22 h to 26 h after `t` (#91); otherwise, for an older Ember or a bad `end`, the next entry's `t` when it is a valid integer within 25 h, read before that entry's own validation, so a malformed middle day does not stretch the day before it (PR #84 review); otherwise unknown. The 22-26 h window takes any real DST shift (up to 2 h) and rejects a value that is not a day's end. An entry that is not an object, has no integer `t`, misses or has an out-of-range `min_c`/`max_c`, has `min_c` > `max_c`, or does not come after the day before is skipped; the first three good ones are kept. `precip_mm10` is not read (AN1 shows the chance only). Units: Ember sends metric only and the knob has no unit setting, so there is no °F. No protocol change from the knob: `end` is additive on Ember's side, and the knob still works without it.
- **Face order** (`wx_faces.h`): `WXF_TEMP`, `WXF_RAIN`, `WXF_CAST`, `WXF_WIND`, `WXF_DAYS`; five face dots. A turn reaches it on any weather. Never the auto face: it is a browse face, and nothing in the current weather asks for it.
- **Rows** (`components/weather/wx_days.c`, pure, host-tested):

  | State | Face |
  |---|---|
  | Days from Ember | One row per day that has not ended by the knob's clock (`wx_knob_now`: the observation time plus its age; a day ends at its `end`, so a 23 h or 25 h DST day ends at local midnight, e.g. Belgrade 2026-03-29 and 2026-10-25; with the end unknown, 24 h after its `t`), centred on y 228 with AN1's 85 px pitch (143/228/313 for three, 186/270 for two, 228 for one) |
  | Knob clock unknown (no `observed_at` or server time) | Every day Ember sent |
  | Stale or old (> 30 min) data | The same rows: a forecast for tomorrow stays good for hours, and dropping ended days handles a view that stops after midnight. Offline, the sub-line under the rows shows the observation's age (y 372) |
  | No days left, no `days`, disabled, no data | "--" grey in the value slot, no rows |

  - The last row ends at Ember's `end` (#91), so it too ends at local midnight on a DST day. Only with an Ember older than v0.53.1 (no `end`) does it still have no next entry and end 24 h after its `t`: on a DST day an hour off local midnight (23 h day: shown to 01:00; 25 h day: gone at 23:00), only while the knob runs on a view at least two days old. The knob has no time-zone rules.
  - Weekday (`wx_weekday`): `t` + `utc_offset` rounded to the day by adding 12 h, so the name stays right when Ember's offset and `t` disagree by under 12 h (MET's `lon/15` fallback across DST); without an offset the noon-rounded UTC date, right for offsets under ±12 h. English short names ("Sun".."Sat"): the firmware has no localisation.
  - Icons (`wx_days_icon`): clear (day or night) → sun, partly cloudy → sun and white cloud, overcast → grey cloud, fog → grey cloud and two mist lines, rain → grey cloud and three drops, snow → grey cloud and three flakes, storm → dark cloud and a bolt. AN1 draws only the first, second and fifth; the others are mine, in the same style and colours (the Rain face's snow #D0DCE8 and bolt #F0D040, the cloud tones of the Temperature face). The clouds are the canvas's closed path with a 2 px stroke (`wx_cloud_sd`, 0.9.63, #99).
  - The chance (#5C9CE0, 24 px, centred under the icon) shows on rain, snow and storm days when known, as AN1's "70%" on its rain day; a dry day's chance is not shown.
  - Axis: the lowest low to the highest high of the rows shown (a single flat day widens by a degree each way). A span narrower than the bar's height becomes a 10 px dot centred on its value, kept inside the bar.
  - Columns (`wx_days_cols`): AN1's day 60, icon 40, low 54 (right aligned), bar 104, high 54 (left aligned), 10 px gaps, 352 px centred (x 57..409). A wider name ("Mon", "Wed": 68 and 70 px in Montserrat 30) or temperature ("−4°" 60 px, "−25°" 78 px in `font_face_36`) widens its column and the bar gives up the same width, so the row always keeps AN1's 352 px and its corners stay inside the ring (the bar is at least 34 px for any name and -99..99). The widths come from tables in `wx_days.c` (the bake has no fonts); `test_days_label.c` holds them to the real fonts glyph by glyph, kerning included.
- **Rendering** (`wx_paint.c` bake, `main/temp_face.c` LVGL):
  - Background: AN1's thin ring (r 222, 6 px, #262626, through the ring strip as the other faces' gauges), the face dots. Nothing else: the rows are layers so they can arrive.
  - One RGB565A8 sprite per row (`wx_paint_day_row`: the icon, the grey track as a 10 px capsule, the coloured span as a capsule whose colour runs along the temperature scale from the low to the high, as AN1's gradient), all three in the still-cloud buffer (`wx_days_layout`: 3 × 224 × 44 px, 88,704 B of 201,312 B); no new allocation. The icons are drawn in AN1's 40 px viewBox (the canvas's circles and stroke widths), static: AN1's ray sway, cloud drift and falling drops are ambient loops the face-design row rules out ("static after arrival").
  - Text: per row a container holding the sprite and four labels: the name (Montserrat 30, #BDBDBD), the low (`font_face_36` at 80 % opacity, AN1's dim), the high (`font_face_36`), each in its temperature colour, and the chance. AN1 uses 34 px for the temperatures; `font_face_36` (the gauge end labels' subset) is the closest compiled size.
  - A forecast change (any row's day, icon, chance, low or high, or the axis) is a full scene bake through M8's fade; the current weather never re-bakes this face.
- **Arrival** (page open and cut): each row fades in and rises 12 px over 0.42 s, 80 ms after the row above (AN1's `wxrow`, the ease approximated by the arrival's cubic ease-out), while the lows and highs count up on the Temperature face's ease (`wx_count_step`, every step in its own colour, as AN1's count strips). Then nothing moves. The bars appear with their row instead of growing from their centre (AN1's `wxbar` is a live scale).
- **Areas/frame**: one redraw box over the rows and their slide (`area`, 362 × 256 px for three rows, 92,672 px; 362 × 170 for two, 362 × 86 for one): on a frame where any row moves, fades or counts, `set_days` invalidates an invisible object over it and the rows' and labels' own invalidations join it. Each row container is as wide as the box (`wx_days_row_box`; at first it was 466 px wide, which made each frame ~119k px, PR #84 review). `set_days` paints `wx_days_frame`, the host model `test_days.c` walks at 30 fps: every row box, old and new, inside the one box; changes on 25 of the arrival's frames for three rows (23 for two, 18 for one), none after. 1 area per changed frame at up to 30 fps for 1.32 s, then none: no ambient motion, so nothing redraws until the next forecast change or the burn-in step. Larger than the other faces' boxes (Wind 19k px, Rain now 3 areas, 35k px) but only during the arrival; the stats log on the knob gives its ms.
- **Bake time**: host (M-series, -O1) full scene 0.4-2.6 ms over 7 runs (`test_days.c` prints it). S3 0.16-0.17 s (0.9.61 log, `tempface: baked parts 7 ... face 4 ...`).
- **Cost** (`idf.py size`, origin/main 0f7d858 (0.9.56) → this branch, temporary worktree and this worktree, same machine, IDF 5.5.5, `sdkconfig` from `sdkconfig.defaults`):

  | | origin/main | 0.9.57 | Δ |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Image (`cinder.bin`) | 1,637,152 B | 1,645,888 B | +8,736 |
  | Flash `.text` / `.rodata` | 1,102,824 / 430,860 B | 1,111,176 / 431,244 B | +8,352 / +384 |
  | IRAM `.text` | 15,356 B | 15,356 B | 0 |
  | DIRAM static (`.data` / `.bss`) | 102,071 B (23,464 / 15,384) | 101,919 B (23,464 / 15,232) | −152: +76 B (19 object pointers), +64 B (`wx_obs_t` grew in `s_store`), −360 B (the legacy poller's `static wx_obs_t obs` moved to PSRAM: used only by its own task, PR #84 review) |
  | PSRAM `.bss` | 50,844 B | 51,564 B | +720 (`wx_days_t` in the job's keys, layout, label texts, shown state; the legacy `obs`, 424 B) |
  | PSRAM heap, weather page shown / hidden | 906,798 B / 0 | same | 0: the row sprites live in the still-cloud buffer |
  | Internal heap (LVGL objects, for life) | | 19 more objects (the box, 3 rows, 3 sprites, 12 labels) | estimate ~4 KB, not measured |
  | `stack_walk.py` | `lvgl` 8,592 (margin 1,904), `wxbake` 2,528 (margin 1,568), `ember` 5,152 (margin 1,760) | same; `weather` 3,952 of 5,120 (margin 1,168, its `obs` is static, not on the stack) | 0 |

- **Deviations from AN1**: the fifth dot (the canvas's "6 of 6" counts faces not built yet); temperatures in 36 px, not 34; columns widen for wide names and temperatures (AN1's fixed 60/54 px columns would let "Wed" and "−25°" run into the icon); the bars appear with their row instead of growing from the centre; no ambient icon motion; the overcast, fog, snow and storm icons, the dot for a flat day and the states AN1 does not show (fewer days, no days, ended days) are mine.
- **Tests**: `test_days.c` (weekdays with and without the offset, at ±12-14 h, the noon rounding's edge, before 1970, names wrapping; condition to icon for every face and outside the list, which are wet; rows for AN1 and the fixture, the chance only on wet days (0 % shown, none unknown, over 100 clamped, fog dry), two days, one flat day, none, a negative or too large count, clamped and swapped ranges, no data, invalid, disabled, stale and old data keeping its days; ended days at the last second and the first, a 25 h day, the last day's 24 h, every day over, a negative age, the clock unknown, the knob's own age; ends kept across a skipped entry and an unknown end as 24 h; Belgrade's 23 h and 25 h days shown to their last second and gone at local midnight; `wx_days_same` field by field and past `n`; bake kinds: nothing, the current weather, a forecast change, face changes; the pick on any weather, never the auto face, picks away clearing the days; row positions for 1-3 rows; spans on AN1's axis, a narrower bar, flat days and short spans at both ends; width tables; AN1's columns, wider name and low columns, frost both ends, the row kept at 352 px and the shortest bar (34 px) over every name and -99..99; layout: sprite margins, offsets, the redraw box, the still buffer for every range, one row, never past the array; the arrival: start, ease, stagger, monotonic, still after; the area model for 1-3 rows: row boxes inside the redraw box, its size, nothing after the arrival, rows as wide as the box, the slide, the frame's counts; the bake: the 6 px ring, black elsewhere, the dot, the track, the span's colours at both ends, its height, the clear low column, every icon's parts, a flat day's dot, no sprite without rows), `test_days_label.c` (with `font_face_36` and LVGL's Montserrat 24 and 30: the width tables against the fonts for -99..99 and the seven names, every chance's glyphs and width, every row from -45 to 45 for every weekday inside the ring's inner edge with 6 px to spare, columns apart, the chance below the temperatures, the offline line under the rows), `test_faces.c` (five faces: order, wrapping, turns, a full turn, the turn base; dots for the Next days face with and without data), `test_fixtures.c` (`view_faces.json` Mon/Tue/Wed clouds, rain, snow on -4..17 and never the auto face; `view_faces_met.json` storm and snow without chances; the polar fixture from Wednesday at +2 h; 11 crafted entries (a number, fractional `t`, min > max, missing and out-of-range values, rounding, a fractional or string chance, a repeated or string `t`, no condition, a fourth good day); `days` not an array or missing; MET night symbols on a day; legacy views without days; each day's end from Ember's `end`, a bad middle day keeping its boundary, ends more than 25 h on or unreadable; Belgrade's 23 h and 25 h last days from `end` shown to their last second and gone at local midnight, the same days without `end` (an older Ember) on the next start and 24 h, an `end` at `t`, before it, 1 s inside or outside 22-26 h, fractional or a string ignored, 22 h and 26 h kept (#91)). `run.sh`: the days attach on a scene apply, their sprites detach with the others and attach only from the still buffer, `paint` shows the baked days, the rows fade with the face, one box redraws, no days painter on the LVGL task; the value hidden under the rows, the sub-line under them, the gauge end labels hidden, the rows placed and sized from the host model (each line killed its mutant, PR #84 review); the legacy `obs` in PSRAM. Mutants killed (95 tried): the weekday rounding, offset use and floor, icon and wet mapping, clamps, the disabled and invalid checks, the count clamps, a day's end and the comparison, the swapped range, the chance's wet gate, bound and clamp, `have`, the axis min, max and widening, every `wx_days_same` field and the `n` bound, the row pitch, the span's minimum, its centring and both clamps, the width tables, each column's widening, the centring, the sprite margins and offsets, the box's slide and margins, the arrival's stagger, ease and slide, the ring width, the icons' clouds, tones, bolt, sun, rays, drop slant, flakes and mist, the span's colour direction, the track, the dot and hero outputs, the sprite's row offset, the parser's order, range, chance type, count, night flag and the call itself, the bake kind and the pick. Equivalent ones were removed from the code (a clock-known test, a non-object check and a memset that changed nothing); one on the Temperature hero's sun gradient (pre-existing code) survives the days tests only.
