## Sun face (#86, #34 part 5, 0.9.58)

The sixth weather face (canvas AS2, [`face-design.md`](face-design.md) row "Sun"): a day gauge from dawn to dusk, the marker at now, daylight left as the value, the next sun event in the sub-line, sunrise and sunset as the end labels, and a sun rising behind a horizon with a fan of rays. Same pipeline as the other weather faces: `wx_job` bake on `wxbake`, images only while shown, OTA hold, M8 fade on a new day, cut and arrival on a turn, face dots baked, burn-in shift. **Not checked on the knob yet.**

- **Data** (Ember#350, in the goldens since 0.9.51; no protocol change): `parse_weather` now keeps `sunrise`/`sunset` as Unix seconds (both or neither: one null or not an integer clears the pair, as Ember sends them both-null or both-set), the six `twilight` crossings (`dawn`, `blue_end`, `golden_end`, `golden_start`, `blue_start`, `dusk`; each unknown unless a positive integer) and Ember's `night` flag even without sun times (`has_sun_down`/`sun_down`, unknown unless a boolean). `wx_obs_t` grows by 72 B.
- **Face order** (`wx_faces.h`): `WXF_TEMP`, `WXF_RAIN`, `WXF_CAST`, `WXF_WIND`, `WXF_DAYS`, `WXF_SUN`; six face dots. Appended after Next days as the face list asks (the canvas has Sun fourth of six). A turn reaches it on any weather. **Never the auto face**: AS2 says nothing about showing itself, and the sun's times are a browse, not an alert. Auto order stays Rain now, Rain forecast (rain within the hour), Wind (39 km/h), Temperature.
- **Crossings** (`components/weather/wx_sun.c`, pure, host-tested): sunrise and sunset anchor the day; a twilight crossing is kept only in order (dawn ≤ blue_end ≤ sunrise ≤ golden_end ≤ golden_start ≤ sunset ≤ blue_start ≤ dusk, equal neighbours kept: Ember rounds to 5 min) and within 12 h of sunrise or sunset; one out of order is dropped, not the ones after it. The gauge runs from the first crossing kept (dawn, else blue_end, else sunrise) to the last (dusk, else blue_start, else sunset): where the sun never reaches −6° or −4° (white nights, Longyearbyen on 1 September in `view_faces_polar_dry.json`) it simply starts and ends at the crossings that happen.
- **Clock and day** (`wx_knob_now`: the observation time plus its age at receipt and since): the value, the phase and the marker need it; without it the face shows "--" and no marker, the gauge and end labels stay. Ember's crossings are for its local day (the solar day around that date's noon; it re-sends at local midnight). The knob takes that day from the crossings themselves, the local date (`utc_offset`) of their solar noon, mid sunrise..sunset, not from when the view arrived: a view built at 23:59:59 and received at 00:00:01 still holds yesterday's times (Codex, PR #88). Sun times whose day is ahead of the knob's clock or more than 7 days behind it count as none ("--"), so a corrupt or far-off timestamp can never reach the countdown, and the countdown itself is capped at 48 h (`WX_SUN_COUNT_MAX_S`), so its count always fits an `int`. Ten minutes past a later local midnight (`WX_SUN_ROLL_GRACE_S`) every crossing moves on by whole days, so the face counts to the next sunrise instead of showing yesterday's evening. The grace is for an online knob: Ember's new view lands within seconds of midnight, so the knob never moves the times itself and then bakes again for Ember's (one M8 fade at midnight, not two; Opus, PR #88); the old view's times show for those ten minutes, after sunset either way. Known limit: the moved times are yesterday's, a few minutes off at mid latitudes and more near the poles, and a DST change moves the clock an hour; both only while the knob runs on a view from an earlier day.
- **States**:

  | Now | Value | Sub-line |
  |---|---|---|
  | Before sunrise | time to sunrise, "h"/"min" | "until sunrise 07:30" #FF9A3D |
  | Sunrise to golden_end (morning golden hour) | daylight left, "h left"/"min left" | "golden hour to 08:30" #FFB547 |
  | golden_end to golden_start | daylight left | "golden hour 18:30" #FFB547 (AS2); "sunset 19:30" without golden_start |
  | golden_start to sunset | daylight left | "sunset 19:30" #FF9A3D |
  | From sunset | the day's daylight, "h"/"min" | "of daylight today" #BDBDBD |
  | No sunrise and sunset, location known | "--" | "Midnight sun" (Ember's night flag false, with the sun and rays) or "Polar night" (true, no sun or rays: `wx_sun_hero`), #BDBDBD; a boundary day with one crossing reads right too: before its lone sunset still midnight sun, after it polar night |
  | No sun times, no location, no offset, no data | "--" grey, the horizon without the sun or rays | none |

  - Offline the sub-line shows the observation's age instead, as on every face (the sun's times stay right; the value keeps counting on the knob's clock).
  - The location is known when `moon.up` is a boolean: Ember sends `up`, `rise` and `set` only with a location, and `sunrise`/`sunset` are null both without one and in polar day or night, so a knob without a location never says "Midnight sun".
  - Value rounding (`wx_sun_round`): from an hour in half hours, floored ("9 h left" from 9:00 to 9:29 left, "8½" with the canvas's ½ glyph); below an hour in whole minutes, rounded up (1 min until the last second). Count-up on the Temperature face's ease (`wx_count_step`; up to 23 steps for long days). Value #E0A030 (AS2), unit #9A9A9A.
  - Times are the location's clock (`utc_offset`), "HH:MM".
- **Rendering** (`wx_paint.c` bake, `main/temp_face.c` LVGL):
  - Background: the ring through the ring strip, coloured along its time axis by `wx_sun_rgb`: AS2's generator (`design/faces/tools/sun.py`) with the real crossings for its fixed minutes: dawn #4A4FC8, blue_end #3E6FD8, sunrise −5 min mauve #9A6A9E, +6 min #E08A5A, +17 min #FF9A3D, golden_end −17 min #FFB547, +17 min the day's edge (on the generator's line from #7E5A20 at golden_end to noon's gold; when golden hour ends within 17 min of noon, as near 60°N in late December, that is noon's gold itself, so the ring never drops to the dark edge at noon: Opus, PR #88), solar noon (mid sunrise..sunset) #B5832A, mirrored in the evening; a stop that falls on or before the one before it is dropped. `test_sun.c` holds 26 colours to the generator's output for AS2's day within 1 per channel. The sunrise and sunset ticks (r 199-210, 2 px, #FF9A3D), the horizon (y 196, 125..341, 4.5 px, #9AA4B0), its two dashes, the face dots. Without sun times: the grey track, no ticks, no end labels, no marker.
  - Rays: one A8 image (330 × 156, recoloured #FFB547), the 13 rays of AS2 with their radial fade and the rim fade baked. Each pixel tests only the ray nearest its angle (the rays are 13° apart, far more than their width), so the bake is cheap (host about 1.2 ms the whole scene); `test_sun.c` holds the image to a brute-force paint of all 13 rays within 1 per pixel.
  - Sun: one RGB565A8 sprite (150 × 90: glow r 74 at 24 %, the disc r 42 in the Temperature face's sun gradient at 90 %), inside a container that ends at y 194, so moving the sprite down hides it behind the horizon without a mask.
  - Both in the still-cloud buffer (91,980 of 201,312 B); no new allocation.
  - A new day's crossings or offset is a full scene bake through M8; the clock moving never re-bakes.
- **Arrival** (page open and cut): the sun rises 64 px on the arrival's ease (AS2's 1.32 s), the rays fade in from 0.72 s at 15 fps (on the first step past 1.44 s), the marker sweeps and the value counts up. Then nothing moves but the marker (about a pixel a minute) and the value. AS2's ray sway (±3° over 9 s) is a live rotation the rendering rules rule out; the rays also do not rise with the sun (they are hidden while it rises).
- **Areas/frame**: the sun moves inside its container (`set_sun` invalidates it, 150 × 90), which lies inside the rays' box, so one area per frame of at most 330 × 156 (51,480 px) while the rays fade; `test_sun.c` walks `wx_sun_frame` at 30 fps: changes on 37 of the arrival's frames (the rays on 12), none after 1.47 s.
- **Cost** (`idf.py size`, origin/main 3ce3e2b (0.9.57) → this branch, this worktree before and after, IDF 5.5.5, `sdkconfig` from `sdkconfig.defaults`):

  | | origin/main | 0.9.58 | Δ |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Image (`cinder.bin`) | 1,645,888 B | 1,658,576 B | +12,688 |
  | Flash `.text` / `.rodata` | 1,111,176 / 431,244 B | 1,123,516 / 431,580 B | +12,340 / +336 |
  | DIRAM static (`.data` / `.bss`) | 101,919 B (23,464 / 15,232) | 102,007 B (23,464 / 15,320) | +88: `wx_obs_t` +72 B in `s_store`, 3 object pointers |
  | PSRAM `.bss` | 51,564 B | 52,020 B | +456 (`wx_sun_t` in the job's keys and the layout) |
  | PSRAM heap, weather page shown / hidden | 906,798 B / 0 | same | 0: rays and sun in the still-cloud buffer; the `wxbake` stack +1,024 B for life |
  | Internal heap (LVGL objects, for life) | | 3 more (the rays, the container, the sun) | estimate ~0.6 KB, not measured |
  | `stack_walk.py` | `lvgl` 8,592 (margin 1,904), `wxbake` 2,528 (margin 1,568), `ember` 5,152 (margin 1,760) | `lvgl` and `ember` same; `wxbake` 2,544 of 5,120 (margin 2,576) | the ray directions are a table, so the bake calls no `sinf` (its first version cost 1,504 B of margin); the `wxbake` stack grows from 4 to 5 KB in PSRAM (`temp_face_create`, `tools/stack_walk.json`): the margin of 1,552 B was 16 B over the 1.5 KB rule (PR #88 review), and every face adds to `wx_bake`'s frame |

- **Deviations from AS2**: six dots, Sun last (the canvas has it fourth); no ray sway; the rays fade in at rest instead of rising with the sun; the states AS2 does not show (before sunrise, the golden hours, after sunset, polar, no data) and their texts are mine.
- **Tests**: `test_sun.c` (AS2 at 10:30: 9 h left, the sub-line, the labels, the marker 215 of 790 min in; every phase and its edges to the second; rounding; the day moving offline at local midnight with the offset as the boundary, two days, missing crossings staying missing, no clock; crossings dropped out of order or past 12 h, kept on sunrise, sunset and equal neighbours; polar day and night, no location, no flag, one sun time, a 24 h day, no offset, no data; the ring colours against AS2's generator, a stop on its predecessor, no crossings; `wx_sun_same_scene` per field; the pick on any weather, never auto, bake kinds: the clock moving, a new day, another face; the arrival's frames, rounding, 15 fps steps, still after; layout boxes, offsets, the still buffer, the fan painted over the whole face staying in its box above the horizon and inside the rim; the area model; the bake: ring ends and noon, ticks, horizon, dashes, dots, the fan, the sun's core and glow, the grey track without ticks), `test_sun_label.c` (every sub-line, value with unit and end label over a day at 5 min steps for offsets −12..+14 h: glyphs in the real fonts, the widest sub-line 317 px "golden hour to 00:30" inside the ring, the widest value 278 px "10½ h left", the end labels' ink inside the ring), `test_fixtures.c` (Ember's sun times, twilight and night flag from `view_faces`, MET and the polar fixture; no location in `view_single_host_paused`; a legacy block; null and malformed sun times and crossings), `test_faces.c` (the six-face list), `run.sh` greps for the LVGL glue (attach from the still buffer, the sun clipped by its container over the rays, layers hidden on other faces, the thermometer hidden, the value, marker and sub-line from `wx_sun_t`, the frames from `wx_sun_frame`, one redraw box, the end labels). Mutation runs: about 160 tried over `wx_sun.c`, the parser and the bake, all killed after the fixes except `wx_sun_frame`'s `+1e-9` and `unix_in`'s `> 0` (equivalent); the equivalent ones in `wx_sun.c` were removed from the code. The `temp_face.c` glue is not host-built: `run.sh` greps the lines whose loss would show (the sun box shown, sized and placed, the rays placed and shown, their opacities, the shown-state reset, the unit and its centring, the end-label texts, `sun_create`, the hero hide), and about 30 mutants of them all fail it; other edits to that file are only as safe as those greps (Opus, PR #88).
- **On the knob** (to do): turn to Sun: compare with AS2 (snapshot); the stats log for the arrival box and that only the marker moves after it; the bake log's ms for a full scene; the end labels against the ring on the left; a day across local midnight while online (Ember's new view re-bakes through M8).

## Air face (#87, #34 part 6, 0.9.59)

The seventh weather face (canvases AA2 and AA4, [`face-design.md`](face-design.md) row "Air"): the European AQI on AA2's gauge, the value with "AQI", the band word in its colour, and a flower that is healthy on a clean day and wilted on a poor one, with one petal falling. Same pipeline as the other weather faces (`wx_job` bake on `wxbake`, images only while shown, OTA hold, M8 fade, cut and arrival on a turn, face dots baked, burn-in shift). Stacked on the Sun face (0.9.58). **Not checked on the knob yet.**

- **Data**: Ember's `aqi` (Ember#350, in the goldens since 0.9.51; no protocol change): sent only while the air tile is on and its reading fresh, so absent means unknown, never clean. `parse_detail` reads it as a number 0..999, rounded (`has_aqi`/`aqi`, +8 B in `wx_obs_t`); a string, a negative or anything above 999 is unknown. Ember sends no pollutants on the knob view, and the face needs none: AA2 and AA4 show the index and its band only.
- **Scale** (`components/weather/wx_air.c`, pure, host-tested): the European AQI bands with the upper edge inclusive (Open-Meteo's `european_aqi`): Good to 20 #4FD6CC, Fair to 40 #50CCAA, Moderate to 60 #F0E641, Poor to 80 #FF5050, Very poor to 100 #D03060, Extremely poor above #B060E0 (mine: the UV scale's purple; AA2's gauge ends at 100). The gauge and the counting value use AA2's continuous colour (`wx_air_rgb`: the band colours at 10, 30, 50, 70 and 90, linear between, rounded half up; held to AA2's generator within 1 per channel). The marker sits at the AQI on 0-100, at the end above 100.
- **Auto face, the full order** (`wx_temp_from_shown`): Rain now (wet) → Rain forecast (rain within 60 min, kept to 90) → Wind (39 km/h, kept to 34) → **Air (AQI above 60, Poor or worse; kept while shown down to 56)** → Temperature. Next days and Sun are never auto. Poor is the threshold because it is where the EEA's health advice starts asking everyone, not only sensitive groups, to consider reducing intense outdoor activity, and where AA4's wilted flower starts; Moderate (AA2) is ordinary air and would make the face show up most days in many cities. Air ranks after the weather faces because rain and wind are what changes the next hour and air quality moves slowly; a poor-air day still shows Air as soon as it is dry and calm. Stale or old data never makes Air the auto face.
- **States**:

  | Data | Face |
  |---|---|
  | AQI up to 60 | AA2: the healthy flower, the value counting up in the gauge colours, the band word |
  | AQI above 60 | AA4: the wilted flower with the ground, three fallen petals, dust and a hazard sign, one petal falling |
  | Flower on screen | Kept within 5 points of 60: the wilted flower stays above 55, the healthy one to 65 (one bake per change, not per hourly wobble) |
  | No `aqi` (air tile off, MET without the air call, reading not fresh), stale or old (> 30 min) data, no data | Grey track, "--", no marker, no band word; the healthy flower and the end icons |

- **Rendering** (`wx_paint.c` bake, `main/temp_face.c` LVGL):
  - Background: the gauge through the ring strip (`wx_paint_ring_air`), the end icons (AA2's outlined flower at the clean end, the hazard triangle at the other, both scaled 1.2), the flower, the face dots. AA2 and AA4 draw the flower with stems, leaves, petals and centre; the bake paints the same shapes with signed distances: the stem a cubic Bézier sampled in 24 steps, a leaf as AA's lens with its half-width along its length, petals as rotated ellipses (AA2's radial petal shading; the distance is the `length(p/r)` approximation, exact enough that a 3 px outline keeps the small end-icon petals open, where the plain gradient one filled them in), all in AA's nested transforms (AA4's whole plant sags 18° and its head turns 32°). The ring goes into the background first and the end icons over it, as on the canvas (blitting it last notched the hazard triangle's corner). `test_air.c` holds the plants, the hazard sign and both end icons to a brute-force reference painter (dense outlines, libm transforms) window by window, with limits a little above today's counts: the plants (off by more than 16 / 40 per channel: 146 / 61 of 5,455 px for AA2, 106 / 20 of 5,382 for AA4, at overlapping petal outlines), AA2's stem foot (0 of 64), the flower end icon (22 / 0 of 560), the hazard end icon and AA4's sign (0 of 766 and 696). It does not pin `shape`'s extra 1 px clip margin, which only adds the faintest fringe.
  - Static, so it costs no redraws: AA2's sway and nod and AA4's sag are live rotations, its dust drifts and its hazard sign pulses in scale. The dust is baked at its resting places at 60 %, the sign at full strength.
  - The falling petal (AA4 only): 6 RGB565A8 sprites of 20 × 20 px, one per 10° step of its turn out of −50°, in the still-cloud buffer (7,200 B); one container over its travel (34 × 78 px) clips and redraws it. `wx_air_petal` is AA4's 7 s loop at 15 fps on its ease-in-out (cubic-bezier 0.42, 0, 0.58, 1), starting 2 s in as the canvas's delay: it drifts down 58 px and 14 px left while turning upright, lies there from 62 % to 90 % of the loop, then fades; hidden during M8's fade like the wind's leaves.
  - Value and unit as the Wind face ("AQI" #9A9A9A); the band word in the sub-line in its band colour.
  - A change of flower or of having data is a full scene bake through M8; the AQI moving inside a flower only moves the marker and the text.
- **Areas/frame**: the healthy flower none after the arrival. The wilted one 1 area of 34 × 78 (2,652 px) on 67 of every 105 frames at 15 fps (`test_air.c` walks two loops: every sprite inside the box, the box exactly its travel, still while the petal lies there).
- **Stack**: the flower's rotations use `wx_deg_cs` (cos and sin in degrees, reduced to ±45° and a Taylor series, within 2e-6 of libm over ±720°) instead of `sinf`/`cosf`, whose large-argument reduction (`__kernel_rem_pio2f`, 400 B) put `wxbake` 288 B over its stack in the first build; the leaf, stem and triangles are distance functions, not 50-point outlines on the stack.
- **Cost** (`idf.py size`, the Sun branch 2f911e0 (0.9.58) → this branch, IDF 5.5.5, `sdkconfig` from `sdkconfig.defaults`):

  | | 0.9.58 | 0.9.59 | Δ |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Image (`cinder.bin`) | 1,658,576 B | 1,667,968 B | +9,392 |
  | Flash `.text` / `.rodata` | 1,123,516 / 431,580 B | 1,132,428 / 432,060 B | +8,912 / +480 |
  | DIRAM static (`.data` / `.bss`) | 102,007 B (23,464 / 15,320) | 102,015 B (23,464 / 15,328) | +8 (`wx_obs_t` in `s_store`, 2 object pointers) |
  | PSRAM `.bss` | 52,020 B | 52,076 B | +56 |
  | PSRAM heap, weather page shown / hidden | 906,798 B / 0 | same | 0: the petals in the still-cloud buffer |
  | Internal heap (LVGL objects, for life) | | 2 more (the petal's box and the petal) | estimate ~0.4 KB, not measured |
  | `stack_walk.py` | `lvgl` 8,592 (margin 1,904), `wxbake` 2,544 of 5,120 (2,576), `ember` 5,152 (1,760) | `lvgl`, `ember` same; `wxbake` 2,528 (2,592) | the worst `wxbake` path is the Temperature face's rays again |

- **Deviations from AA2/AA4**: seven dots, Air last (the canvas has it fifth of six); no sway, nod, sag, drifting dust or pulsing sign (live rotation, scaling or a second moving box); the falling petal turns in 10° steps; Extremely poor's purple, and the grey no-data face, are mine.
- **Tests**: `test_air.c` (AA2's colours and exact half-up rounding; the six bands at their edges, words and colours, clamped; the flower's hysteresis both ways; `wx_air_from`: AA2 and AA4, clamping, the marker above 100, the kept flower and when it is not kept, no aqi, stale, old, invalid, no data; the auto rule at 60/61 and 55/56, stale and missing; the full auto order against Rain now, Rain forecast and Wind; the pick on any weather, picks away clearing it, the kept flower on a pick under another auto face, bake kinds; `wx_air_same_scene` per field; the petal's loop at exact frames of AA4's curve, fade in, rest, fade out, periodic, monotonic, inside its box; `wx_deg_cs` against libm; the bake: gauge ends and middle, no petals on a healthy day, the end icons, AA2's centre, petal shading, stem and leaf, nothing of AA4, both flowers against the reference painter, the hazard sign, ground, fallen petal, dust, the drooping centre, the six petal sprites and their turn, the grey no-data gauge), `test_air_label.c` (every band word and every value 0-999 with "AQI" in the real fonts inside the ring), `test_fixtures.c` (Ember's aqi from `view_faces`, none on MET, polar and legacy; rounded, out of range, a string; a dry calm view at 75 resolving to Air, wilted), `test_faces.c` (the seven-face list), `run.sh` greps for the Air glue. Mutation run: about 90 tried over `wx_air.c`, the parser, `wx_temp.c`, the bake and the `temp_face.c` glue; after the PR #89 review's fixes all killed except the colour scale's `<`/`<=` at a stop (both sides give the same colour), the leaf's slope term and `shape`'s 1 px clip margin (both inside the reference painter's limits); the other equivalent ones (the petal shading's inner branch, under the centre disc) were removed from the code.
- **On the knob** (to do): turn to Air on a clean and a poor day (or the fixtures through a scratch server only with the user's OK): compare with AA2 and AA4 (snapshots); the stats log for the petal's box; the bake log's ms; that Air becomes the auto face on a poor dry day and steps aside for rain and wind.

## Bot weather moments (#90, #34 part 7, 0.9.61)

The last item of #34 ([`face-design.md`](face-design.md) "Bot weather moments (v2)", canvases `B2-*`): the bot page shows a short weather interruption (~4.5 s) above and below the eyes, then the bot is exactly as before. **Not checked on the knob yet.**

- **Pure logic** (`components/weather/wx_moment.c`, host-tested in `test_moment.c`): trigger, priority, cooldowns, texts, the 15 fps timeline, the eye script and the area model the LVGL glue uses itself (`wxm_diff`). The weather comes from the same knob view as the weather faces, through their helpers: `wx_rain_from` (rain, snow, storm, `until`), `wx_wind_strong` (39 km/h, kept to 34), `wx_temp_from` (value, night, cloud band, moon), `wx_temp_sky` (the sky word, new: the sub-line's word without the UV advice). No AQI moment: the design has none.
- **Kinds**: change moments, ranked Storm > Rain > Snow > Rain stops (AfterRain) > Wind > Heat > Frost; and the periodic one, which shows the weather now: Rain, Snow or Storm while it falls, Night on a clear night (cloud band 0-1), else Glance. Frost (≤ 0°, kept to 2°) has no canvas: it plays the Glance sprite and acting with "Frost -3°". Heat from 28° (kept to 26°). Snow stopping has no moment (a rainbow after snow is wrong). Texts as on the canvas, sentence case, ASCII minus (Montserrat 30 has no U+2212): "12° Partly cloudy", "Clear 9°", "Rain until 16:30" (else the rain word), "Snow -2°", "Wind 45 km/h" (Beaufort colour), "Hot 31°", "Frost -3°", "Rain stops", "Storm"; temperatures in the temperature scale's colour, rain #5C9CE0, storm #F0D040. `test_moment_label.c` measures 176,802 texts in the real font: all fit the 300 px line (widest "-40° Mostly sunny", 273 px).
- **When** (`wxm_tick`, every frame, whatever page shows):
  - A change is the signature (precipitation kind, hot, frost, windy, with the hysteresis above) moving between two fresh observations; the first observation after boot is only the baseline. The highest-ranked change of a step queues; a newer change replaces it when it ranks at least as high or the queued one no longer holds. A queued change is dropped when its weather is gone (rain that already stopped) or after 30 min.
  - The periodic moment every `bot.weather_every_min` (default 30, 0 none) while the bot page shows and the bot is not waiting or erroring; it is skipped when its text equals the last moment's (also a change moment's), and a slot missed off the page or while busy starts a full interval later. A new interval reschedules the slot from the change, shorter or longer.
  - Gates: `bot.weather_moments` on (default), fresh data (not stale, < 30 min), the bot page shown and the page and the bot's own mood unchanged for 3 s (idle to done or a wake from sleepy restart it, though both rest), no glint chase (phase not idle), not in quiet hours with calm on, no moment already baking or playing, 5 min since the last moment, 30 min since the same kind (change and periodic moments alike).
- **Priority by mood**: waiting / error: none (changes wait, periodic ones drop). Working: the quiet variant (sprite and text only; the eyes keep reading, the glint keeps orbiting; the tool icon steps aside for the line). Idle, sleepy, done: the full variant with eye acting.
- **Moods and lifecycle**: any mood change or quiet hours cut a moment short (`wxm_abort_frame`: the sprite and the text keep the opacity they have and leave from there, a cut before the first frame ends it; the acting ends with a glide to the viewer, or where the gaze is when the mood changed, so the new mood's own glance stays: `bot_act_drop`); leaving the bot page or the OTA face hides it at once (`moment_view_stop`, `moment_view_hold`; the OTA hold also blocks new bakes, like the rim and `wxbake` jobs). No chase starts while a moment bakes or plays (`glint_chase_t.hold`: the working time still counts, the chase starts after it).
- **Eye acting** (`bot_act_*` in `bot_behavior.c`, host-tested in `test_bot.c`): while acting the bot makes no saccades of its own and ignores a knob turn (`bot_look` would hold the eyes up in the sprite box until the next cue) and a push (its hop moves the whole outline); the acting gazes keep the body lean the pre-roll set (as for the viewer), so the outline never moves (otherwise a glance up of 0.25 leans it 2 px, a full outline redraw); a costume (round for Snow, happy for Heat and AfterRain) swaps in at the close of the blink it starts and survives a mood change; a lid floor eases in and out; AfterRain's hop is the normal hop. The cues are bot2.py's scripts, pinned in `test_script_design`. **Pre-roll**: the full variant first glides the eyes back to the viewer and plays 0.5 s later (`WXM_PRE_S`), because the idle and done rest gaze (up right) puts an eye into the sprite box; the canvas starts at the viewer. `test_acting` runs the real bot (idle, done, sleepy, working; 40 start times each) through every kind: the eyes never enter the sprite box while it shows (top 159.4 px, box ends at 152) nor the text line while it shows (bottom 305.7 px, line from 310). Without the pre-roll it fails.
- **Rendering** (`main/moment_view.c`): the arena and the `moment` task are allocated on the first start, the arena freed again when moments are turned off and nothing bakes or plays. One container at the sprite box (158,62)-(312,152) on the bot page (the canvas's (156,62)-(308,152) moved 3 px right for the 472 px frame, even-aligned) holding up to 8 RGB565A8 canvases, and a 300 × 36 px label for the line. The sprites are painted with signed distances (`wx_moment_paint.c`: circles, capsules, flattened quadratic/cubic/arc paths, the clouds' outline path as the canvas draws it (`wx_cloud_sd`, shared with the faces since 0.9.63, #99)) at the canvas's 0.9 scale into a 96 KB PSRAM arena (Rain's three umbrella poses need 81,831 B) by a `moment` task (core 1, prio 2, 4.5 KB stack in PSRAM) when a moment starts; it claims the job by CAS and publishes after the paint, the LVGL task attaches the canvases after that. Ambient motion is element positions and opacities on the 15 fps grid (rain drops, flakes, gusts, leaf, glow pulse, twinkle, drift, umbrella poses, storm flashes, rainbow fade), sampled from the canvas's keyframes. Moon phase as on the Temperature face (no hemisphere flip).
- **Areas/frame** (`wxm_diff`, the same function the glue uses; `test_areas` walks every kind and every abort point): a frame redraws the sprite box (154 × 90 = 13,860 px, one invalidate before its children change) or the text line (300 × 36 = 10,800 px; on the quiet variant's two icon frames the line and the icon as one box), never both. While a moment bakes or plays the two eye canvases are invalidated as one box (`bot_view_chase` merge, as in a chase). `test_acting` models the bot view's invalidations per 60 fps frame on the real bot (eyes as one area when the pose changes, the outline when its even-pixel offset or squash changes, the moment's area, the glint step when working) and checks ≤ 2 areas full, ≤ 3 quiet, and no outline move outside AfterRain's hop. Sprite frames per moment: Glance/Frost 28, AfterRain 27, Storm 18, the others 62 (their motion runs every frame); the text 6. With the eyes that is ≤ 2 areas per frame (full) and ≤ 3 with the glint (quiet), the design's budget; the AfterRain hop is the documented exception (the whole outline, as in any hop).
- **Settings**: `bot.weather_moments` (bool, absent on) and `bot.weather_every_min` (0-240, absent 30) in `knob_settings`, sent by Ember since Ember#443 to every knob.
- **Caps** (#100, Ember#450, 0.9.65): `wx_moments` is the seventh feature token, so Ember.app shows the two settings only for firmware that applies them (it showed them for every knob before, and they did nothing before 0.9.61; 0.9.61-0.9.64 apply them but report no token, so the app hides the rows there until the knob updates). Ember's registry has the token; `checkin_req_caps.json` keeps 0.9.41's five tokens, so `test_fixtures.c` lists it in `FEATURES_AHEAD` next to `wx_faces` (no fixture sync). The worst-case checkin body grows by 13 B to 1,776 of 2,048 B (`test_caps.c`).
- **Decisions** (open in #34): icon size: the canvas's 0.9 scale in its 152 × 90 box, measured against the eye boxes above. Moon: only when Ember's `moon.up` is true (as the Temperature face, #33); a clear night without it shows the two stars only.
- **Cost** (`idf.py size`, origin/main 0.9.59 at 504d051 → this branch rebased on 0.9.60 at 694207d, IDF 5.5.5; the image delta includes #92's small change):

  | | 0.9.59 | 0.9.61 | Δ |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Image (`cinder.bin`) | 1,667,968 B | 1,691,552 B | +23,584 |
  | DIRAM static | 102,015 B | 102,167 B | +152 (`.bss` +136, `.data` +16) |
  | PSRAM `.bss` | 52,076 B | 53,412 B | +1,336 (the trigger and its `wx_temp_t` scratch) |
  | PSRAM heap | | +96 KB arena while moments are on; +4.5 KB `moment` stack (and its TCB) from the first moment on, kept when they are turned off | 0 until the first moment |
  | Internal heap | | 10 LVGL objects | estimate ~2 KB, not measured: 1,180 B of object structs (DWARF: 8 × `lv_canvas_t` 128, `lv_obj_t` 48, `lv_label_t` 108), plus the box's child array and `spec_attr`, local styles, the label text and allocator headers (`CLIB_MALLOC`, below `SPIRAM_MALLOC_ALWAYSINTERNAL`) |
  | `stack_walk.py` | `lvgl` 8,592 (margin 1,904), `ember` 5,152 (1,760) | same; new `moment` 2,864 of 4,608 (margin 1,744) | 0 |

  `stack_walk.json` gains the infeasible pair `__ssvfscanf_r` → `ieee80211_*`: in this link the walker kept `__ssvfscanf_r`'s whole sweep and decoded padding after a `j` as a `call8` into the Wi-Fi blob, which put `ember` at 5,728 (margin 1,184) without any change to its code.
- **Stats log**: `weather moments: N shown, last bake X ms` every 30 s, and an error with the count of starts that found no arena or bake task (the LVGL task does not log). A failed start is not counted as shown: the trigger offers it again (`wxm_tick` offers, `wxm_shown` records) and the view retries the allocation after 5 s.
- **Bake yield and cost** (#109, 0.9.67): 0.9.66 reset three times in ten minutes (2026-10-12, `task_wdt`, IDLE1). All three core dumps have the `moment` task (core 1, prio 2) in the Rain bake: `wxm_paint` → `umbrella` → `fill` → `path_sd` → `seg_d` → `hypotf`. The bake never blocked, so IDLE1 got no CPU for the 5 s idle timeout, and the server re-sent the demo after each reboot (the once-per-id memory is RAM), so it looped. Rain moments are rare (`WXM_GAP_S`, `WXM_SAME_S`); demo mode (#106) made one easy to hit.
  - **Yield**: `wxm_paint` takes a hook (`void (*yield)(void)`, NULL for none), called after the arena clear and before every row of every pass, so at most one layer row (≤ 154 px) of work runs between two calls; the painter stays pure. `moment_view.c` `bake_yield` sleeps one tick (`vTaskDelay(1)`) once `MV_YIELD_US` (25 ms) have passed since the bake started or last slept, so the core-1 tasks get a tick at least every ~25 ms of bake. A tick goes to a ready prio-1 task (`orbit`, `link`) before IDLE1 (prio 0), so IDLE1 runs once those block. At boot `orbit` is filling (~24 ms CPU per chunk before its own `vTaskDelay(1)`): under a bake it gets only the bake's ticks (`vTaskDelay(1)` at 1 kHz sleeps 0-1 ms), so one chunk takes ~1.2 s and IDLE1 runs when a chunk ends inside a bake tick; a `wxbake` at the same time (prio 2, no yield, 0.16-0.54 s) time-slices with `moment`. Worst case ~1-2 s without IDLE1, under the 5 s timeout, and the bake itself is estimated at 0.07-0.24 s. That is the case in the dumps: the demo is re-sent at boot while `orbit` is alive. The stats log's `last bake X ms` includes those ticks (~4 %).
  - **Paths**: every path (umbrella poses, bolt, flake arms, streaks, leaf, stars, drop) used to be a signed distance to every segment at every pixel of its layer: the open umbrella is 49 segments over ~8,900 px, a `hypotf` each, three poses. `fill_path` now visits only the path's box grown by its half-width + 1 design px. Per row it lists the segments whose y range is within that reach of the row and the polygon's crossings (the same expression as before, now once per row). Per pixel it skips a near segment by x first, compares squared distances, and takes one `hypotf` of the nearest when it lies within the reach; beyond it the coverage is 0 or 1 whatever the exact distance, so it uses the reach.
  - **Other elements**: the glows, sun discs, moon, clouds and rainbow loop over their ink box only (radius + 1-2 px; the cloud's puffs down to its base, grown by the stroke + 1; the bow's ring down to its centre line). Outside it every `put` was a no-op (alpha ≤ 0.002).
  - **Same pixels**: byte-identical on the host. `test_moment_paint.c` includes a verbatim copy of the 0.9.66 painter (`test/host/ref_moment_paint.c`, its three public names renamed by macro) and compares layouts and arenas for every kind with and without the sun or moon, and the moon at every illumination 0-100 waxing and waning (its only inputs are `phase < 50` and the clamped illumination), on a cleared and a 0xFF arena. On the S3, FMA contraction can move the SDF math by an ulp (as in #95), so there the claim is "no visual change". The test also holds the hook: at least one call per layer row and at most one layer row of pixels evaluated between two calls, in every pass, and a NULL hook paints the same. The rows are counted, not the changed bytes (a transparent pixel or a repeated opaque write still costs its SDF): `design_at`, which every pass calls for each pixel it evaluates, calls `WXM_PIXEL(im, y)`, a no-op on the device; the host test build defines it (`-include test/host/moment_pixel.h`) to record the layer and row, and the test counts the distinct rows between two hook calls. Mutants in each of the five per-row passes (`fill`, `glow`, `fill_path`, `sun_disc`, `moon`): a call every 4 rows, and two calls every 2 rows (same call count), each fail it (8-438 failures); dropping the `WXM_PIXEL` call fails it too (no pixel evaluated). The longest interval on the S3 is not measured. `run.sh` greps the wiring: `moment_bake_task` stamps `s_yield_at = t0` and then makes the only `wxm_paint(&s_m, &s_l, s_arena, bake_yield)` call; `bake_yield` returns before `MV_YIELD_US`, sleeps one tick, then restamps; `MV_YIELD_US` is 20-50 ms. Passing NULL, dropping the delay or the stamp, or a 200 ms budget each fail it.
  - **Host bench** (M-series, `CINDER_BENCH=1` `test_moment_paint`, -O2, best of 30, 3 runs; -O1 within 5 %), 0.9.66 painter → 0.9.67:

    | Kind | 0.9.66 | 0.9.67 | × |
    |---|---|---|---|
    | Rain | 4.16-4.19 ms | 0.095-0.097 ms | 43 |
    | Heat | 0.28-0.29 | 0.053-0.054 | 5.3 |
    | Wind | 0.145-0.151 | 0.024-0.027 | 5.8 |
    | Night (moon) | 0.127-0.134 | 0.060-0.065 | 2.1 |
    | AfterRain | 0.167-0.172 | 0.115-0.117 | 1.5 |
    | Storm | 0.137-0.140 | 0.099-0.101 | 1.4 |
    | Glance (sun) | 0.079-0.087 | 0.070-0.075 | 1.2 |
    | Frost (sun) | 0.087 | 0.074 | 1.2 |
    | Snow | 0.036-0.037 | 0.033-0.034 | 1.1 |

    What is left is mostly the clouds (`wx_cloud_sd`, shared with the faces) and the rainbow's five rings.
  - **S3 estimate** (not measured): none of the three dumps had finished the old Rain bake after 5 s, against 4.2 ms on the host, so this SDF-heavy code runs ≥ ~1,200× slower there; the face bakes measured ~340-1,000× (Temperature 0.51-0.54 s against 0.5-1.6 ms, Rain now 1.68 s against 2.2-2.6 ms, both -O1). At 700-2,000×: Rain 0.07-0.2 s, the slowest kind now (AfterRain, 0.12 ms) 0.08-0.24 s, plus ~4 % of yield ticks. The full variant's 0.5 s pre-roll covers that; a quiet one starts when the bake lands. The stats log gives the real number.
  - **Other bake tasks on core 1** (checked, unchanged): `rim` (prio 2) sleeps a tick after each outline variant (~0.12 s CPU each, task watchdog section). `wxbake` (prio 2) does not yield inside `wx_bake`: one face bake keeps IDLE1 off core 1 for its whole length, 0.16-0.54 s on the S3 (0.9.61 log; Rain now 1.68 s before #95, cheaper since). That is under the 5 s timeout, so no reset, but it is the same pattern; a hook like this one is the fix if a face bake ever grows.
  - **Cost** (`idf.py size`, origin/main 0.9.66 at fae9659 → this branch, same machine, IDF 5.5.5): image 1,700,087 → 1,702,191 B (+2,104, the row-list path filler and the boxes inlined into the passes); DIRAM 102,303 B unchanged; PSRAM `.bss` +8 (`s_yield_at`), the `moment` stack (PSRAM) 4,608 → 5,120 B. `stack_walk.py`: `moment` 2,800 of 4,608 → 3,424 of 5,120 (margin 1,808 → 1,696; at the old 4,608 it would be 1,184, under the 1.5 KB rule, so the stack grows by 512 B; one unresolved call left, counted as 0 B): `fill_path` keeps the row's segment and crossing lists on its frame (496 B), and `stack_walk.json` now lists `bake_yield` and the fill SDFs as the targets of the painter's indirect calls (unresolved 2 → 1, tabled 7), so the walk also counts `bake_yield` → `vTaskDelay` and `fill` → `cloud_sd` → `wx_cloud_sd`, which the old estimate counted as 0 B.
- **On the knob** (to do): the bake ms; the stats log's refresh times on sprite frames; snapshots against the B2 canvases; a moment while working (glint, label, icon), a mood change mid-moment, page switch and OTA during one.

## Demo mode (#106, Ember#457, 0.9.66)

Ember plays scripted scenes on the knob (the bot moods, the weather moments and faces, a chase, Pomodoro and now playing, offline states) for screenshots and showing the knob off. Ember owns the scene list (Ember `docs/DEVICE-PROTOCOL.md`, demo mode); the knob only plays what the view carries. **Not checked on the knob yet.**

- **Wire**: the view gains an optional `demo` block `{id, page, view?, act?, offline?, hold}`. `page` is `bot`, `pomodoro`, `nowplaying`, `weather` or `weather:<face>` (temp, rain, cast, wind, days, sun, air). `view` is a complete view (same schema, no nested demo) that stands in for the real one while the scene plays. `act` is `moment:<kind>:full|quiet` (glance, night, frost, heat, wind, afterrain, snow, rain, storm), `chase:full|half`, `hop`, `pop`, `blink`, `sleepy` (the bot sleepy for the scene, 8 s) or `asleep` (asleep for the scene, 8 s, since 0.9.69); an unknown act plays the scene without it, a block without a usable `id` (1-64 bytes) or `page` counts as absent. The knob reports on every view request: `demo_id=<id>&demo_state=playing|done` (percent-encoded), only while the server sends that id; it keeps reporting `done` until the block goes away. Feature token `demo` (eighth; in `FEATURES_AHEAD` until Ember's goldens list it); the worst-case checkin body grows by 7 B to 1,783 of 2,048 B.
- **Pure logic** (`components/demo`, host-tested in `test_demo.c`): `demo_block_parse`, `demo_act_parse`, `demo_page_parse`, and the state machine `demo_t`: an id plays at most once while it is among the last 8 ids (Ember makes a new id for every scene, so the ring only guards a re-delivered id); a new id ends a playing one and starts; a block that goes away ends it (`hold` too). Timing counts from `demo_ready` (the scene's view applied, before the poll task fetches now-playing art, which may arrive late as for a real view), not from the offer, and a scene never ready ends 5 s after it: a view-only scene 8 s; an act its natural length, at least 8 s when it brings a view, 60 s at most; `hold` until the server clears it or 60 s. An act's completion carries its scene's `gen`, so a late one from a replaced scene changes nothing. Any input ends it. `demo_mood`, `demo_calm`/`demo_quiet_dim` and the input gate `demo_gate` are here too, so the glue is host-tested. `knob_view_read_full` parses the block and its view in the one parse (`knob_view_step` keeps both on a 304); fixtures: four hand-made scenes in `test/host/fixtures/demo/` and Ember's 120 scenes in `fixtures/ember/demo/` (`sync-ember-fixtures.sh` takes dotted names such as `moment.storm.full.json` under `demo/`).
- **Poll task** (`ember_client.c`): every applied view offers its block to `demo_t` (under a spinlock; the LVGL task reads it too). While a scene plays, `apply_view` feeds the pages (host label, Pomodoro, weather, now playing) from the scene's view and keeps the real epoch, live mode and brightness (on purpose: the room's light, not the scene's, sets the panel); quiet dimming is off while a scene plays, and calm follows the scene view's `quiet` (whatever the calm setting; none for a scene without a view); the published mood is the scene's, or the mood at its start for a scene without a view, so a real mood change mid-scene shows only after it. When the scene ends (the LVGL task's tick, an input), the poll task is woken (`ember_client_wake`, and `longpoll_idle`/`wait_and_fade` return on a new `gen`), puts the real view back without a request (before the next poll too, so that poll is not aborted at once) and reports `done` on the next one.
- **LVGL task** (`main.c`): a started scene stops a running moment, jumps to its page even when the page is off (`pages_show`, without changing the order; `pages_resume` after) and picks its weather face (`temp_face_pick`, the previous pick put back after). Acts: a moment through `wxm_moment_from` and `moment_view_start` with its eye script, without `wxm_tick`, so `WXM_GAP_S`/`WXM_SAME_S` and the trigger's signature are untouched; `wxm_tick` does not run while the scene's view is applied, so the demo weather never reaches the trigger. A chase through the scene's own request (a server `s_chase_req` mid-scene waits until the scene ends and the real chase is back) with the glint forced on in any mood and with the working ring off (`bot_view_glint_force`; the round outline still needed). Every scene snapshots the real chase and the half chase's blink and snap state at its start, stops a running one, and puts both back at its end as if no time had passed (`gc_restore`: phase, working time, wait, label shift, rng); other scenes hold the chase, so a working-mood scene starts none and a real chase orbiting before it pauses and goes on after. `hop`, `pop`, `blink`: `bot_hop`, `bot_pop`, `bot_blink`, past calm and acting. `sleepy`: the bot sleepy while the scene shows, woken after (`bot_wake`) unless it was asleep before. An act that cannot start within 3 s (a moment off the bot page or without weather data, a chase without the glint) counts as done. Input: from the offer on, every input goes to `demo_gate`, not the page; the input that ends a scene and the rest of its gesture are dropped until the button and the screen are up, 300 ms pass without input and the real view is back; a new scene clears that (`demo_gate_start`), so its own natural end still holds its page. A scene ending on its own keeps its page for 1.5 s, so the next scene of a queue follows without the user's page in between; an input or a server stop brings the user's page back at once. A settings change mid-scene keeps the user's page (`pages_settings` follows `order[pos]`, not the page shown). `offline` makes `ember_client_offline()` true while the scene plays: the bot's link glyph, the Pomodoro and now-playing offline notes and the weather face's observation age.
- **Cost** (`idf.py size` and `stack_walk.py`, origin/main 8b489f9 → this branch, IDF 5.5.5):

  | | 0.9.65 | 0.9.66 | Δ |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Image | 1,693,479 B | 1,700,079 B | +6,600 |
  | DIRAM static | 102,167 B | 102,263 B | +96 (`.data` +16, `.bss` +80) |
  | PSRAM `.bss` | 53,412 B | 54,868 B | +1,456 (`demo_t`, the scene's block, the chase snapshot, moment and `wx_temp_t` scratch) |
  | `stack_walk.py` | `lvgl` 8,592 (margin 1,904), `ember` 5,152 (1,760) | `lvgl` 8,608 (1,888), `ember` 5,088 (1,824) | +16 / -64 |

  The poll context in PSRAM grows by a second `knob_view_t` (the scene's view) and the query buffers.
- **On the knob** (to do): every Ember scene once Ember#457 ships; an input mid-scene; a hold cleared by the server; a chase in an idle mood; a moment with moments off.

## Invalidated-area count (#118)

- **What**: the 30 s serial stats log adds one line per window, `invalidated: <areas> areas (<n>/s), <px> px (<n> px/s) in <refreshes> refreshes over <s> s`, next to the per-kind `refresh <kind>` lines. Both come from `components/refr_stats` (host-tested in `test_refr.c`; `run.sh` checks the `refr_cb` wiring).
- **What counts**: the areas LVGL actually draws. `refr_cb` reads the display's invalid areas at `LV_EVENT_RENDER_START`, which LVGL 9.5 sends after `lv_obj_update_layout` and `lv_refr_join_area`, and counts only the areas not joined into another, so a moved object's new position (invalidated during layout) is counted and joined areas count once, as their bounding box. As stored by `lv_inv_area`: an area fully inside an already stored one is dropped, a partly overlapping one is kept (its overlap counts twice unless the join merges them), and past 32 areas (`LV_INV_BUF_SIZE`) the list collapses to one full-screen area, so the busiest frames read 1 area of 472x466 px.
- **Two refresh counts**: `screen refreshes N` on the fps line counts every `LV_EVENT_REFR_READY`, including refreshes with nothing to draw; `in N refreshes` and the per-kind counts include only refreshes that drew at least one area.
- **Change to existing values**: before #118 the per-kind lines read the areas at `LV_EVENT_REFR_START`, before layout and the join, so a move counted only its old position and a refresh whose only change was a move from off-screen or a hidden position counted nothing. Now per-kind `n`, `avg px` and `max areas` (serial only) include move destinations and count joined areas once, and such refreshes also count for `ota_client_note_frame` (the OTA health gate's render check, never reported). Ember's `fps`, `frame_ms_avg` and `frame_ms_max` come from `diag_note_frame`, which still sees every refresh: unchanged.
- **Window**: refresh accounting and the stats task's take share one spinlock (`s_refr_mux`), and the take records its own time, so the per-second rates use the time between two takes. A refresh counts in the window it ends in.
- **Checkin** (0.9.72, Ember v0.58.0, Ember#473): at full diagnostics the checkin `stats` carries `inv_areas` and `inv_px`, the window totals Ember turns into `invalidated_areas_per_sec` and `invalidated_px_per_sec` in its stats points, so the per-mood numbers in #111, #112 and #113 ("Invalidated areas/s") no longer need USB. The sync commit for v0.58.0 listed `.stats.inv_areas` and `.stats.inv_px` in `NOT_YET_SENT`; this change sends them and removes the entries.
- **Checkin window**: `refr_stats` keeps a second accumulator (`refr_win_t`) next to the per-kind one, fed by the same `refr_stats_end` under `s_refr_mux`. The serial take never touches it. `diag.c`'s `window_start` reads the clock and resets the accumulator in one `s_refr_mux` section and uses that reading as the window start; `diag_sample` reads the clock (the `now` behind `period_ms`) and the totals in one section too. A refresh is accounted under the same lock, so each one lands on one side of both stamps, and the totals are exactly the refreshes accounted between the window start and `now`, the span `period_ms` reports. The sample is a read, not a take: a checkin that fails keeps accumulating into the same window, like `fps`. `app_main` runs `refr_stats_init` and `diag_attach_refr` before the ember task starts, so the first window is measured too. The fields are left out at basic, without a window (`no_window`), and when the accumulator's start differs from the window's (not measured over the whole window). Values are 64-bit (`inv_px` over a 1 h window can pass 2^32); negatives clamp to 0.
- **Between sample and commit**: refreshes that end while the checkin is in flight (after `diag_sample`, before `diag_commit` starts the next window on success) fall in no report, and the time falls in no `period_ms` either, as for `fps`, `req_*` and `cpu_pct`. Ember turns the totals into rates by dividing by `period_ms` and merges points weighted by `period_ms` (Ember `devices_stats.go`, ARCHITECTURE "Knob diagnostics"), so the gap leaves the rates unbiased; the reports just are not contiguous, each gap lasting one request. Carrying those refreshes into the next window would count them against a `period_ms` that does not include their time and bias the rates up.
- **Wiring checks** (`run.sh`): the attach comes before `ember_client_start` and the `refr_cb` registration; `window_start` stamps and resets, and `diag_sample` stamps and reads, each in one `s_refr_mux` section (the clock read must sit in the same section as the reset or read, and each body assigns `now` exactly twice, there and in the no-`s_refr` `else` branch and nowhere else, so splitting them into two back-to-back sections or re-reading the clock after the section fails); `has_inv`, `inv_areas` and `inv_px` are set only inside `diag_sample`'s full-diagnostics block (brace-depth range that also ends at the block's own `} else`, so moving them below the block or into an else branch fails). `refr_cb` and `stats_task` each have one `s_refr_mux` section, and `refr_stats_end`, `refr_stats_take` and the `s_refr_count`/`s_refr_total_us`/`s_refr_max_us` counters appear only inside it; outside their declarations, those counters appear nowhere else in `main.c`.
- **Checkin buffer**: two keys cost 23 B plus digits, which broke `test_caps.c`'s 256 B headroom at 2,048 B, so `DEV_CHECKIN_BODY_MAX` is 2,304 B (PSRAM, `P->body`). The worst-case body (both totals at `INT64_MAX`) is 1,844 of 2,304 B.
- **Cost** (`idf.py size` against main at c253309): image +928 B, DIRAM +64 B; the area copy for the count (512 B) is in PSRAM. Per refresh: one pass over at most 32 areas and a short critical section. `stack_walk.py`: `lvgl` unchanged (8,608 B), `stats` 2,688 → 2,672 B.
- **Checkin cost** (`idf.py size` against main at 7dbe45e): image +548 B, DIRAM +32 B, PSRAM +256 B (the checkin buffer). Per refresh: two more 64-bit adds inside the existing critical section; per checkin window: two short `s_refr_mux` sections on the ember task. `stack_walk.py`: every task unchanged (`stats` 2,672 B, `ember` 5,104 B, `lvgl` 8,608 B).
- **On the knob** (0.9.72, Ember v0.58.0, 2026-10-13 ~18:55 UTC): each bot mood held with Devices › Demo (`bot.<mood>`, hold), Ember live stats at 5 s, 8 samples per mood from 6 s after the scene started; medians (range). Releases before 0.9.72 have no counter, so there is no per-mood baseline; the simulated deltas in the #111, #112, #113 and #127 sections stand.

  | Mood | Areas/s | kpx/s | fps | Core 0 |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | idle | 21.8 (11.8–37.6) | 79 | 11.9 | 18.5 % |
  | working | 39.8 (35.0–45.4) | 286 | 26.9 | 28.1 % |
  | waiting | 20.8 (14.3–29.7) | 1,071 | 14.1 | 39.5 % |
  | done | 10.6 (7.9–14.4) | 113 | 10.8 | 26.0 % |
  | error | 38.7 (30.6–50.3) | 122 | 20.5 | 26.8 % |
  | sleepy | 25.8 (18.8–30.7) | 43 | 15.4 | 17.5 % |
  | asleep | 4.1 (3.3–4.6) | 4 | 2.6 | 7.8 % |

  Waiting moves the most pixels (the hop moves the whole outline, ~1 MB/s) at a moderate area count; working and error redraw the most areas (eyes every saccade, two areas per eye redraw); asleep is near idle for the panel, as #112 aimed for (−7 points of core 0 overnight predicted; 7.8 % measured against idle's 18.5 %).
